## Best date plans

### 2026-09-17 — Gage & Tollner + FUMBL Fest

**Dinner, then FUMBL Fest.** American steakhouse and seafood at Gage & Tollner. It is 1.9 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. FUMBL Fest starts at 7 pm, so book the table early.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=372+Fulton+St%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11201&destination=290+Conover+St%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11231&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://ticketstripe.com/fumblfest) · [Restaurant](https://www.gageandtollner.com/)

### 2026-09-17 — Sofreh + Performance: Forest of Mononoke

**Dinner, then Performance: Forest of Mononoke.** Persian at Sofreh. It is a 15-minute walk from the venue. Performance: Forest of Mononoke starts at 7:30 pm, so book the table early.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=75+St.+Marks+Ave%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11217&destination=573+Sackett+St%2C+2+West%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11217&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://luma.com/theritualdivision) · [Restaurant](https://www.sofrehnyc.com/)

### 2026-09-17 — Sailor + Illumination Light Art Festival

**Dinner, then Illumination Light Art Festival.** Bistro at Sailor. It is 2.5 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. Illumination Light Art Festival starts at 7 pm, so book the table early.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=228+DeKalb+Ave%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11205&destination=20+Battery+Pl%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10280&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.illumination.nyc) · [Restaurant](https://www.sailor.nyc/) · [Reservations](https://www.sailor.nyc/reserve)

### 2026-09-16 — Tatiana by Kwame Onwuachi + The Quiet Beekeeper | New Nordic Cinema

**Dinner, then The Quiet Beekeeper | New Nordic Cinema.** New York-inspired Afro-Caribbean at Tatiana by Kwame Onwuachi. It is 1.6 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. The Quiet Beekeeper | New Nordic Cinema starts at 7 pm, so book the table early.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=10+Lincoln+Center+Plaza%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10023&destination=58+Park+Ave%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10016&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.scandinaviahouse.org/event/the-quiet-beekeeper-new-nordic-cinema/) · [Restaurant](https://www.tatiananyc.com/)

### 2026-09-16 — Gather Round: Let’s Dance with Sekou McMiller + Friends + Mary's Fish Camp

**Gather Round: Let’s Dance with Sekou McMiller + Friends, then dinner.** Seafood at Mary's Fish Camp. It is 1.4 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. It starts at 5 pm, so eat afterwards.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=385+9th+Ave%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10001&destination=64+Charles+St%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10014&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://manhattanwestnyc.com/event/gather-round-lets-dance-2026/2026-09-16/) · [Restaurant](https://www.marysfishcamp.com/)

### 2026-09-17 — Eyval + Gen Z vs Millennials Gameshow

**Dinner, then Gen Z vs Millennials Gameshow.** Iranian at Eyval. It is a 13-minute walk from the venue. Doors 6:30 pm — eat first.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=25+Bogart+St%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11206&destination=167+Graham+Ave%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11206&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.brooklyncc.com/show-schedule/gen-z-vs-millennials-sept-17) · [Restaurant](https://www.eyvalnyc.com/)

### 2026-09-17 — Sofreh + Forest of Mononoke Performance

**Dinner, then Forest of Mononoke Performance.** Persian at Sofreh. It is an 18-minute walk from the venue. Forest of Mononoke Performance starts at 7:30 pm, so book the table early.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=75+St.+Marks+Ave%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11217&destination=The+Ritual+Division&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://luma.com/theritualdivision) · [Restaurant](https://www.sofrehnyc.com/)

### 2026-09-17 — Superiority Burger + Facts Machine Science, Comedy & Trivia!

**Dinner, then Facts Machine Science, Comedy & Trivia!.** Vegetarian diner at Superiority Burger. It is a 9-minute walk from the venue. Facts Machine Science, Comedy & Trivia! starts at 7 pm, so book the table early.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=119+Avenue+A%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10009&destination=21+Clinton+Street&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://caveat.nyc/events/facts-machine-science-comedy--trivia-9-17-2026) · [Event booking](https://www.caveat.nyc/event/facts-machine-science-comedy--trivia-9-17-2026) · [Restaurant](https://www.superiorityburger.com/)

4:00 PM · museums · Free · Museum of Modern Art · Midtown · Manhattan · Score 46.7  
MoMA offers free admission on Friday evenings. Free tickets are limited, released in advance, and go quickly.
**Timing:** Free Friday evenings, roughly 4-8pm. Confirm the current window and ticket release on moma.org.
**Practical note:** Book the free timed ticket the moment it is released; the line for walk-ups is long.
**Why now:** A standing weekly free window at a museum that is otherwise one of the most expensive in the city.

#### [Queens Night Market](https://queensnightmarket.com/)

4:00 PM · markets · Free entry; most dishes capped at a few dollars · Queens Night Market · Corona · Queens · Score 53.4  
Open-air night market behind the New York Hall of Science with about a hundred independent food, art, and merchandise vendors, deliberately kept low-priced.
**Timing:** Saturdays 4pm to midnight. Note the seasonal gap between late August and mid September.
**Practical note:** Outdoors and cash-friendly; the adjacent lot charges for parking, so the 7 train is easier.
**Why now:** Runs every Saturday evening in season; the 7 train to 111th St drops you four blocks away.
[Official](https://queensnightmarket.com/)

## Where to eat

Checked the candidates against captured public evidence and kept useful, verifiable NYC dining items while removing duplicates, sold-out or insufficiently current claims, and heatmap-only wording where it overstated recency.

Dining research: 2026-09-16T10:38:52+00:00

No candidate was verified as a current NYC Michelin-starred restaurant. El Califa de León’s official site/Eater support Michelin stars for the original Mexico City taqueria only, not the NYC location; Kirbee’s source mentions Michelin nods for the Texas pitmasters’ prior restaurants, not Kirbee’s; James Beard titles are from official JBF event pages.

### Express Lunch menu

Deals & happy hours — Hawksmoor New York

Useful weekday-and-Saturday set lunch at a higher-end steakhouse, with steak-and-fries and other mains available as one-, two-, or three-course options.

Price: 1 course $25; 2 courses $40; 3 courses $55  
Schedule: Monday-Saturday, 12:00 PM-3:00 PM  
Conditions: Restaurant page states raw/undercooked/allergen warning; tax, tip, and service-charge details not found in fetched menu text; reservations available but requirement unknown.  
[Source](https://thehawksmoor.com/us/wp-json/wp/v2/pages/7851)

### Ci Siamo aperitivo bar-and-lounge menu

More restaurants — Ci Siamo

Short weekday aperitivo window with snackable Italian items such as pizza bianca, mortadella, pizzette, and gnocco fritto, useful before a later dinner plan.

Price: Aperitivo examples checked: Mortadella con Pistacchio $14; Pizza Bianca $15; Gnocco Fritto $16; Pizzette $16; Caramelized Onion Torta $27  
Schedule: Monday-Friday, 3:00 PM-4:30 PM, bar and lounge  
Conditions: Available in bar and lounge per official menu; reservation requirement, tax, tip, and service-charge details unknown.  
[Source](https://www.cisiamo.com/menus/)

### Lunch prix-fixe availability

Special menus — Crown Shy

The official lunch menu explicitly says a prix-fixe menu is available, alongside seasonal dishes such as Ora King salmon, pork katsu sandwich, and chicken kofta.

Price: Prix-fixe price unknown; a la carte lunch entrees checked from $27-$49  
Schedule: Lunch; exact days/hours not shown in fetched menu text  
Conditions: Official menu confirms prix-fixe availability but did not expose price, course count, tax/tip/service charge, or reservation requirement in fetched content.  
[Source](https://www.crownshy.nyc/menus/)

### Celebration cakes with advance notice

Special menus — Gage & Tollner

Restaurant-owned special-order cake menu is useful for birthdays or group dinners without bringing dessert from outside.

Price: 6-inch cake $95; 8-inch cake $145; 10-inch cake $190  
Schedule: Available for onsite celebrations; order timing depends on advance-notice condition  
Conditions: At least 72 hours notice required; onsite only; tax, tip, service charge, cancellation terms, and reservation requirement not found in fetched menu text.  
[Source](https://www.gageandtollner.com/gage-and-tollner-menus/)

### Daily midday bar-bites menu

More restaurants — Union Square Cafe

A separate daily midday menu fills the gap between lunch and dinner with smaller dishes, raw bar, pastas, and a burger.

Price: Examples checked: Duck Liver Profiterole $6; Garlic Potato Chips $9; Spicy Tuna Tater Tots $12; USC Seafood Platter $56  
Schedule: Daily, 2:30 PM-5:00 PM  
Conditions: This is a separate midday menu, not a stated discount; reservation requirement, tax, tip, and service charge unknown.  
[Source](https://www.unionsquarecafe.com/menus/)

### Midday apéro menu

More restaurants — Cafe Chelsea

The Hotel Chelsea bistro has a separate midday apéro-style menu with lower-entry snacks and seafood options, useful for off-peak eating.

Price: Examples checked: Tapenade $9; Panisse $9; Croquette de Chèvre $12; Rillettes de Saumon $15; Huîtres half-dozen $27  
Schedule: Midday; exact hours not exposed in fetched menu text  
Conditions: Separate menu, not a stated discount; tax, tip, service charge, and reservation requirement unknown.  
[Source](https://www.cafechelseanyc.com/menus/)

### El Califa de León’s Flatiron taqueria

More restaurants — El Califa de León

Mexico City taqueria’s New York debut is tightly focused on four tacos—gaonera, costilla, bistec, and chuleta—plus drinks; useful for a simple Flatiron stop.

Schedule: Monday-Sunday, 11:00 AM-11:00 PM  
Conditions: Official site confirms the NYC address, daily 11 AM-11 PM hours, OpenTable link, and four core tacos; prices were not exposed in captured official text. Michelin-star language applies to the original Mexico City taqueria, not the NYC location.

Barbs B Q’s Chuck Charnichart and Goldee’s Jonny White teamed up on a daytime barbecue spot serving two pitmaster points of view, from spiced brisket and green spaghetti to classic brisket, turkey, and potato salad.

Schedule: Daytime/lunch-focused; first day 11:00 AM-2:00 PM; full opening Labor Day weekend Friday-Sunday per Eater report
